==================================================
FILE: ./chat_bash-script.sh
==================================================

cat > export-project.sh <<'EOF'
#!/usr/bin/env bash

set -uo pipefail

OUTPUT="angular-project-context.txt"
SCRIPT_NAME="$(basename "$0")"

echo "Starting Angular project export..."

# Avoid exporting into itself
: > "$OUTPUT"

write() {
    printf '%s\n' "$*" >> "$OUTPUT"
}

# Exclusion rules
excluded() {
    local path="${1,,}"

    [[ "$path" == *"/node_modules"* ]] && return 0
    [[ "$path" == *"/.git"* ]] && return 0
    [[ "$path" == *backup* ]] && return 0
    [[ "$path" == *backoup* ]] && return 0

    return 1
}

# Text-based extensions
is_text() {
    case "${1##*.}" in
        ts|js|jsx|tsx|html|css|scss|sass|less|json|md|txt|yml|yaml|xml|svg|sh|py|java|cs|sql|config|properties|gradle|env|gitignore)
            return 0 ;;
        *)
            case "$(basename "$1")" in
                Dockerfile|Makefile|Procfile|LICENSE|NOTICE)
                    return 0 ;;
            esac
            return 1 ;;
    esac
}

write "=================================================="
write "ANGULAR PROJECT EXPORT"
write "Project: $(basename "$PWD")"
write "Generated: $(date)"
write "=================================================="

# Collect files ONCE
echo "Scanning project files..."

mapfile -d '' FILES < <(
    find . \
        \( -type d \( -name node_modules -o -name .git -o -iname '*backup*' -o -iname '*backoup*' \) -prune \) \
        -o -type f -print0
)

TOTAL=${#FILES[@]}

echo "Found $TOTAL files."

# 1. DIRECTORY STRUCTURE
echo "Writing project structure..."

write ""
write "=================================================="
write "1. PROJECT STRUCTURE"
write "=================================================="

find . \
    \( -type d \( -name node_modules -o -name .git -o -iname '*backup*' -o -iname '*backoup*' \) -prune \) \
    -o -print >> "$OUTPUT"

# 2. FILE CONTENTS
echo "Exporting source files..."

write ""
write "=================================================="
write "2. FILE CONTENTS"
write "=================================================="

COUNT=0
EXPORTED=0

for file in "${FILES[@]}"; do

    COUNT=$((COUNT + 1))

    [[ "$file" == "./$OUTPUT" ]] && continue
    [[ "$(basename "$file")" == "$SCRIPT_NAME" ]] && continue

    if is_text "$file"; then

        # Skip files that are likely binary
        if ! LC_ALL=C grep -Iq . "$file" 2>/dev/null; then
            continue
        fi

        write ""
        write "=================================================="
        write "FILE: $file"
        write "=================================================="

        cat "$file" >> "$OUTPUT"
        write ""

        EXPORTED=$((EXPORTED + 1))
    fi

    if (( COUNT % 100 == 0 )); then
        echo "Processed $COUNT / $TOTAL files..."
    fi

done

# 3. SKILLS REFERENCES
echo "Checking skills asset references..."

write ""
write "=================================================="
write "3. SKILLS ASSET REFERENCES"
write "=================================================="

if [[ -d "src/assets/skills" ]]; then
    find src/assets/skills -maxdepth 1 -type f -print >> "$OUTPUT"
else
    write "WARNING: src/assets/skills directory not found."
fi

write ""
write "References found in source files:"

for file in "${FILES[@]}"; do
    [[ "$file" == "./$OUTPUT" ]] && continue
    [[ "$(basename "$file")" == "$SCRIPT_NAME" ]] && continue

    if is_text "$file"; then
        grep -nHiE 'assets/skills/|skills/' "$file" 2>/dev/null |
            sed "s|^|$file:|" >> "$OUTPUT" || true
    fi
done

# 4. SUMMARY
write ""
write "=================================================="
write "4. EXPORT SUMMARY"
write "=================================================="
write "Total files discovered: $TOTAL"
write "Text files exported: $EXPORTED"
write "Excluded: node_modules, .git, backup, backoup"
write "Output: $OUTPUT"
write "Completed: $(date)"

echo ""
echo "=========================================="
echo "EXPORT COMPLETE"
echo "=========================================="
echo "Files discovered: $TOTAL"
echo "Text files exported: $EXPORTED"
echo "Output: $OUTPUT"
echo "Size: $(du -h "$OUTPUT" | cut -f1)"
EOF

chmod +x export-project.sh
bash export-project.sh


==================================================
FILE: ./dump-src-app.sh
==================================================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# dump-src-app.sh
# Run from the project root (e.g. ~/my-portfolio-website).
# Produces a single text file with the project structure and the
# contents of every file under src/app (skipping names containing "backup").

OUTPUT="project-context.txt"
TARGET_DIR="src/app"

if [ ! -d "$TARGET_DIR" ]; then
  echo "Error: '$TARGET_DIR' not found. Run this script from the project root."
  exit 1
fi

{
  echo "=============================================="
  echo " PROJECT CONTEXT"
  echo " Generated: $(date)"
  echo "=============================================="
  echo ""

  echo "----------------------------------------------"
  echo " 1. PROJECT STRUCTURE (src/app)"
  echo "----------------------------------------------"
  if command -v tree >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    tree -I "*backup*" "$TARGET_DIR"
  else
    find "$TARGET_DIR" -print | grep -vi "backup" | sed -e "s|[^/]*/|  |g"
  fi
  echo ""

  echo "----------------------------------------------"
  echo " 2. FILE CONTENTS"
  echo "----------------------------------------------"
  find "$TARGET_DIR" -type f -not -iname "*backup*" | sort | while read -r file; do
    echo ""
    echo "=============================================="
    echo " FILE: $file"
    echo "=============================================="
    cat "$file"
    echo ""
  done
} > "$OUTPUT"

echo "Done. Output written to: $OUTPUT"

    description: string;

    description: 'A lightweight developer productivity tool with syntax highlighting, searchable snippets, categorisation and code-sharing functionality.',
    projectLink: 'https://github.com/example/code-snippet-manager',
    tags: [Tag.REACT, Tag.JAVASCRIPT, Tag.PYTHON],
    pictures: []
  },
  {
    id: 5,
    name: 'InventoryPro',
    summary: 'An inventory management system for tracking products and stock levels.',
    description: 'A business-oriented inventory platform with product management, stock alerts, supplier records, reporting and role-based access control.',
    projectLink: 'https://github.com/example/inventory-pro',
    tags: [Tag.ANGULAR, Tag.TYPESCRIPT, Tag.ASPNET, Tag.CSHARP],
    pictures: []
  },
  {
    id: 6,
    name: 'AI Document Assistant',
    summary: 'An intelligent assistant that answers questions about uploaded documents.',
    description: 'A document intelligence application using retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to process uploaded files, generate embeddings and provide context-aware answers.',
    projectLink: 'https://github.com/example/ai-document-assistant',
    tags: [Tag.PYTHON, Tag.REACT, Tag.NODEJS],
    pictures: []
  },
  {
    id: 7,
    name: 'Java Banking API',
    summary: 'A RESTful banking API for managing accounts and financial transactions.',
    description: 'A backend banking service supporting account creation, balance enquiries, deposits, withdrawals, transaction histories and validation.',
    projectLink: 'https://github.com/example/java-banking-api',
    tags: [Tag.JAVA, Tag.ASPNET],
    pictures: []
  },
  {
    id: 8,
    name: 'EventHub',
    summary: 'An event discovery and booking platform for local communities.',
    description: 'A full-stack event management application where users can discover events, reserve tickets, manage bookings and receive event notifications.',
    projectLink: 'https://github.com/example/eventhub',
    tags: [Tag.ANGULAR, Tag.TYPESCRIPT, Tag.NODEJS],
    pictures: []
  },
  {
    id: 9,
    name: 'DevOps Monitor',
    summary: 'A dashboard for monitoring application health and deployment activity.',
    description: 'A monitoring dashboard that aggregates application metrics, deployment status, service availability and operational logs into a centralised interface.',
    projectLink: 'https://github.com/example/devops-monitor',
    tags: [Tag.REACT, Tag.TYPESCRIPT, Tag.PYTHON],
    pictures: []
  }
];

  constructor() { }

  getProjects(){
    return this.projects;
  }

  getProjectById(id: number): Project {
    let project = this.projects.find(project => project.id === id );

    if(project === undefined){
      throw new TypeError('There is no project that matches the id: ' + id);
    }

      return project;
  }

  getProjectsByFilter(filterTags: Tag[]): Project[] {

    if (filterTags.length === 0) {
        return this.getProjects();
    }

    return this.projects.filter(project =>
        project.tags.some(projectTag =>
            filterTags.includes(projectTag)
        )
    );
  }
}

 FILE: src/app/portfolio/portfolio.component copy.ts

import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { Title } from '@angular/platform-browser';
import { Project } from '../_models/Project';
import { Tag } from '../_models/Tag';
import { ProjectsService } from '../_services/projects.service';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-portfolio',
  templateUrl: './portfolio.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./portfolio.component.css']
})
export class PortfolioComponent implements OnInit{

  projects = {} as Project[];

  filtering : boolean = false;

  isCollapsed: boolean = true;
  typescript: boolean = false; 
  python: boolean = false; 


  constructor(private titleService: Title, private projectService: ProjectsService){
      this.titleService.setTitle('Afrika Funda - Portfolio')
  
    }

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.projects = this.projectService.getProjects();
  }

  filter(){
    let filterTags: Tag[] = [];

    if(this.typescript){
      filterTags.push(Tag.TYPESCRIPT);
    }
    if(this.python){
      filterTags.push(Tag.PYTHON);

    }

    if(this.python || this.typescript)
    {
      this.filtering = true;
    }
      else {
      this.filtering = false;
    }

    this.projects =this.projectService.getProjectsByFilter(filterTags);
  }

  resetFilters(){
    // add all projects
    this.typescript = false;
    this.python = false;
    this.filtering = false;


    this.projects = this.projectService.getProjects();
  }
}

==============================================
 FILE: src/app/portfolio/portfolio.component.ts
==============================================
import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { Title } from '@angular/platform-browser';
import { Project } from '../_models/Project';
import { Tag } from '../_models/Tag';
import { ProjectsService } from '../_services/projects.service';

@Component({
    selector: 'app-portfolio',
    templateUrl: './portfolio.component.html',
    styleUrls: ['./portfolio.component.css']
})
export class PortfolioComponent implements OnInit {

    projects: Project[] = [];

    filtering: boolean = false;
    isCollapsed: boolean = true;

    // Available technology filters, grouped by category.
    filterGroups = [
        {
            name: 'Languages & Web',
            tags: [
                Tag.JAVA,
                Tag.TYPESCRIPT,
                Tag.JAVASCRIPT,
                Tag.PYTHON,
                Tag.CSHARP,
                Tag.HTML,
                Tag.CSS
            ]
        },
        {
            name: 'Frontend',
            tags: [
                Tag.ANGULAR,
                Tag.REACT
            ]
        },
        {
            name: 'Backend & Frameworks',
            tags: [
                Tag.NODEJS,
                Tag.NESTJS,
                Tag.ASPNET,
                Tag.SPRING_BOOT,
                Tag.FLASK
            ]
        },
        {
            name: 'Databases & ORM',
            tags: [
                Tag.MONGODB,
                Tag.SQL,
                Tag.PRISMA
            ]
        },
        {
            name: 'DevOps & Cloud',
            tags: [
                Tag.DOCKER,
                Tag.KUBERNETES,
                Tag.AZURE,
                Tag.ORACLE_CLOUD
            ]
        },
        {
            name: 'AI & LLM Technologies',
            tags: [
                Tag.LANGCHAIN,
                Tag.LANGGRAPH,
                Tag.MCP,
                Tag.CREWAI
            ]
        }
    ];

    // Tracks the selected state of each technology checkbox.
    selectedTags: Record<string, boolean> = {};

    constructor(
        private titleService: Title,
        private projectService: ProjectsService
    ) {
        this.titleService.setTitle('Afrika Funda - Portfolio');
    }

    ngOnInit(): void {
        this.projects = this.projectService.getProjects();
    }

    filter(): void {
        const filterTags: Tag[] = [];

        // Collect all selected technologies.
        for (const group of this.filterGroups) {
            for (const tag of group.tags) {
                if (this.selectedTags[tag.toString()]) {
                    filterTags.push(tag);
                }
            }
        }

        this.filtering = filterTags.length > 0;

        // Apply filters, or show all projects when none are selected.
        this.projects = this.filtering
            ? this.projectService.getProjectsByFilter(filterTags)
            : this.projectService.getProjects();
    }

    resetFilters(): void {
        this.selectedTags = {};
        this.filtering = false;

        this.projects = this.projectService.getProjects();
    }
}

import { Component, Input } from '@angular/core';
import { Project } from '../_models/Project';
import { BsModalRef, BsModalService, ModalOptions } from 'ngx-bootstrap/modal';
import { ProjectModalComponent } from '../project-modal/project-modal.component';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-project-card',
  templateUrl: './project-card.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./project-card.component.css']
})
export class ProjectCardComponent {
  @Input() project = {} as Project;
  bsModalRef?: BsModalRef;

  constructor(private modalService: BsModalService){

  }

   OpenProjectModal(){
    const modalOptions: ModalOptions = {
      class: "modal-lg",
      initialState: {
        project: this.project
      }
  };

      this.bsModalRef = this.modalService.show(ProjectModalComponent, modalOptions);
    }
}

 FILE: src/app/resume/resume.component.ts
==============================================
import { Component, Renderer2} from '@angular/core';
import { Title } from '@angular/platform-browser';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-resume',
  templateUrl: './resume.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./resume.component.css']
})
export class ResumeComponent { 

    isWorkExperienceOpen: boolean = false;
    isEducationOpen: boolean = false;
    isCertificationsOpen: boolean = false;
    IsSkillsOpen: boolean = false;

    constructor(private titleService: Title, private renderer: Renderer2){
        this.titleService.setTitle('Afrika Funda - Resume')
    
      }
    downloadFile(){
      const link = this.renderer.createElement('a');
      link.setAttribute('target', '_blank');
      link.setAttribute('href', '../..assets/resume.pdf');
      link.setAttribute('download, Resume.pdf');
      link.click();
      link.remove();
    }

}

FILE: ./README.md
==================================================
# MyPortfolioWebsite

This project was generated with [Angular CLI](https://github.com/angular/angular-cli) version 15.2.11.

## Development server

Run `ng serve` for a dev server. Navigate to `http://localhost:4200/`. The application will automatically reload if you change any of the source files.

## Code scaffolding

Run `ng generate component component-name` to generate a new component. You can also use `ng generate directive|pipe|service|class|guard|interface|enum|module`.

## Build

Run `ng build` to build the project. The build artifacts will be stored in the `dist/` directory.

## Running unit tests

Run `ng test` to execute the unit tests via [Karma](https://karma-runner.github.io).

## Running end-to-end tests

Run `ng e2e` to execute the end-to-end tests via a platform of your choice. To use this command, you need to first add a package that implements end-to-end testing capabilities.

## Further help

To get more help on the Angular CLI use `ng help` or go check out the [Angular CLI Overview and Command Reference](https://angular.io/cli) page.

FILE: ./src/app/app-routing.module.ts

import { NgModule } from '@angular/core';
import { RouterModule, Routes } from '@angular/router';
import { HomeComponent } from './home/home.component';
import { PortfolioComponent } from './portfolio/portfolio.component';
import { ResumeComponent } from './resume/resume.component';
import { ContactComponent } from './contact/contact.component';


const routes: Routes = [
  {path: 'home', component: HomeComponent}, 
  {path: 'portfolio', component: PortfolioComponent}, 
  {path: 'resume', component: ResumeComponent}, 
  {path: 'contact', component: ContactComponent}, 
  {path: '**', component: HomeComponent, pathMatch: 'full'}, 
];

@NgModule({
  imports: [RouterModule.forRoot(routes)],
  exports: [RouterModule]
})
export class AppRoutingModule { }

import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { Title } from '@angular/platform-browser';
import { Project } from '../_models/Project';
import { ProjectsService } from '../_services/projects.service';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-home',
  templateUrl: './home.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./home.component.css']
})
export class HomeComponent implements OnInit {

  featuredProject = {} as Project;
  constructor(private titleService: Title, private projectService: ProjectsService){
    this.titleService.setTitle('Afrika Funda - Home')

  }
  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.featuredProject = this.projectService.getProjectById(0);
  }


}

FILE: ./src/app/portfolio/portfolio.component copy.ts

import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { Title } from '@angular/platform-browser';
import { Project } from '../_models/Project';
import { Tag } from '../_models/Tag';
import { ProjectsService } from '../_services/projects.service';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-portfolio',
  templateUrl: './portfolio.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./portfolio.component.css']
})
export class PortfolioComponent implements OnInit{

  projects = {} as Project[];

  filtering : boolean = false;

  isCollapsed: boolean = true;
  typescript: boolean = false; 
  python: boolean = false; 


  constructor(private titleService: Title, private projectService: ProjectsService){
      this.titleService.setTitle('Afrika Funda - Portfolio')
  
    }

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.projects = this.projectService.getProjects();
  }

  filter(){
    let filterTags: Tag[] = [];

    if(this.typescript){
      filterTags.push(Tag.TYPESCRIPT);
    }
    if(this.python){
      filterTags.push(Tag.PYTHON);

    }

    if(this.python || this.typescript)
    {
      this.filtering = true;
    }
      else {
      this.filtering = false;
    }

    this.projects =this.projectService.getProjectsByFilter(filterTags);
  }

  resetFilters(){
    // add all projects
    this.typescript = false;
    this.python = false;
    this.filtering = false;


    this.projects = this.projectService.getProjects();
  }
}

    <div class="mb-4">
        <div>

                (click)="isCollapsed = !isCollapsed">
                Filter
            </button>

            <button
                class="btn btn-outline-secondary mb-2"
                (click)="resetFilters()"
                [hidden]="!filtering">
                Reset
            </button>
        </div>

        <div [collapse]="isCollapsed" [isAnimated]="true">
            <div class="panel filter-panel" style="max-width: 420px;">
                <div class="panel__titlebar">
                    <span class="dot dot--red"></span><span class="dot dot--yellow"></span><span class="dot dot--green"></span>
                    <span class="panel__titlebar-label">filters.ts</span>
                </div>

                <div class="panel__body">
                    <div *ngFor="let group of filterGroups" class="mb-3">

                        <small class="filter-group-label">
                            <b>{{ group.name }}</b>
                        </small>

                        <div
                            *ngFor="let tag of group.tags"
                            class="form-check d-flex align-items-center gap-2 my-2">

                            <input
                                class="form-check-input"
                                type="checkbox"
                                [id]="'filter-' + tag.toString()"
                                [name]="'filter-' + tag.toString()"
                                [(ngModel)]="selectedTags[tag.toString()]"
                                (ngModelChange)="filter()">

                            <label
                                class="form-check-label d-flex align-items-center gap-2"
                                [for]="'filter-' + tag.toString()">

                                <span class="icon-box-xs">
                                    <img [src]="tag.imageUrl" [alt]="tag.toString()">
                                </span>

                                <small>{{ tag.toString() }}</small>

                            </label>
                        </div>

                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Project cards -->
    <div class="row">

        <div
            *ngFor="let project of projects"
            class="col-lg-4 col-md-6 mb-4"
            style="max-width: 380px; min-width: 300px;">

            <app-project-card [project]="project"></app-project-card>

        </div>

    </div>

</div>

FILE: ./src/app/portfolio/portfolio.component.ts
==================================================
import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { Title } from '@angular/platform-browser';
import { Project } from '../_models/Project';
import { Tag } from '../_models/Tag';
import { ProjectsService } from '../_services/projects.service';

@Component({
    selector: 'app-portfolio',
    templateUrl: './portfolio.component.html',
    styleUrls: ['./portfolio.component.css']
})
export class PortfolioComponent implements OnInit {

    projects: Project[] = [];

    filtering: boolean = false;
    isCollapsed: boolean = true;

    // Available technology filters, grouped by category.
    filterGroups = [
        {
            name: 'Languages & Web',
            tags: [
                Tag.JAVA,
                Tag.TYPESCRIPT,
                Tag.JAVASCRIPT,
                Tag.PYTHON,
                Tag.CSHARP,
                Tag.HTML,
                Tag.CSS
            ]
        },
        {
            name: 'Frontend',
            tags: [
                Tag.ANGULAR,
                Tag.REACT
            ]
        },
        {
            name: 'Backend & Frameworks',
            tags: [
                Tag.NODEJS,
                Tag.NESTJS,
                Tag.ASPNET,
                Tag.SPRING_BOOT,
                Tag.FLASK
            ]
        },
        {
            name: 'Databases & ORM',
            tags: [
                Tag.MONGODB,
                Tag.SQL,
                Tag.PRISMA
            ]
        },
        {
            name: 'DevOps & Cloud',
            tags: [
                Tag.DOCKER,
                Tag.KUBERNETES,
                Tag.AZURE,
                Tag.ORACLE_CLOUD
            ]
        },
        {
            name: 'AI & LLM Technologies',
            tags: [
                Tag.LANGCHAIN,
                Tag.LANGGRAPH,
                Tag.MCP,
                Tag.CREWAI
            ]
        }
    ];

    // Tracks the selected state of each technology checkbox.
    selectedTags: Record<string, boolean> = {};

    constructor(
        private titleService: Title,
        private projectService: ProjectsService
    ) {
        this.titleService.setTitle('Afrika Funda - Portfolio');
    }

    ngOnInit(): void {
        this.projects = this.projectService.getProjects();
    }

    filter(): void {
        const filterTags: Tag[] = [];

        // Collect all selected technologies.
        for (const group of this.filterGroups) {
            for (const tag of group.tags) {
                if (this.selectedTags[tag.toString()]) {
                    filterTags.push(tag);
                }
            }
        }

        this.filtering = filterTags.length > 0;

        // Apply filters, or show all projects when none are selected.
        this.projects = this.filtering
            ? this.projectService.getProjectsByFilter(filterTags)
            : this.projectService.getProjects();
    }

    resetFilters(): void {
        this.selectedTags = {};
        this.filtering = false;

        this.projects = this.projectService.getProjects();
    }
}

import { Component, Input } from '@angular/core';
import { Project } from '../_models/Project';
import { BsModalRef, BsModalService, ModalOptions } from 'ngx-bootstrap/modal';
import { ProjectModalComponent } from '../project-modal/project-modal.component';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-project-card',
  templateUrl: './project-card.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./project-card.component.css']
})
export class ProjectCardComponent {
  @Input() project = {} as Project;
  bsModalRef?: BsModalRef;

  constructor(private modalService: BsModalService){

  }

   OpenProjectModal(){
    const modalOptions: ModalOptions = {
      class: "modal-lg",
      initialState: {
        project: this.project
      }
  };

      this.bsModalRef = this.modalService.show(ProjectModalComponent, modalOptions);
    }
}

.resume-accordion {
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: var(--radius);
  overflow: hidden;
}

/* ngx-bootstrap renders accordion-group content via internal bootstrap
   classes, so these need ::ng-deep to reach them. */
::ng-deep accordion-group .card,
::ng-deep accordion-group .card-body {
  background: var(--bg-panel-raised);
  border-color: var(--line);
  color: var(--text-hi);
}

::ng-deep accordion-group .btn-link {
  color: var(--text-hi);
  text-decoration: none;
  font-family: var(--font-mono);
}

::ng-deep accordion-group .card-header {
  background-color: var(--bg-panel);
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
}

.chevron {
  width: 16px;
  font-family: var(--font-mono);
  color: var(--cyan);
  text-shadow: 0 0 8px rgba(0, 240, 255, .5);
}

.resume-entry {
  text-align: left;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 2px;
}
.resume-entry .icon-box-lg {
  margin-bottom: 10px;
}
.resume-entry ul { color: var(--text-lo); }

.skill-group-heading {
  font-size: 1rem;
  color: var(--cyan);
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 1px;
  margin-bottom: 10px;
}

FILE: ./src/app/resume/resume.component.ts
==================================================
import { Component, Renderer2 } from '@angular/core';
import { Title } from '@angular/platform-browser';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-resume',
  templateUrl: './resume.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./resume.component.css']
})
export class ResumeComponent {

    isWorkExperienceOpen: boolean = false;
    isEducationOpen: boolean = false;
    isCertificationsOpen: boolean = false;
    IsSkillsOpen: boolean = false;

    constructor(private titleService: Title, private renderer: Renderer2) {
        this.titleService.setTitle('Afrika Funda - Resume')
    }

    downloadFile() {
      const link = this.renderer.createElement('a');
      link.setAttribute('target', '_blank');
      link.setAttribute('href', '../../assets/resume.pdf');
      link.setAttribute('download', 'Resume.pdf');
      link.click();
      link.remove();
    }

}

import { Tag } from "./Tag";

export interface Project{
    id: number;
    name: string;
    summary: string;
    description: string;
    projectLink: string;
    pictures: string[];
    tags: Tag[];
}

  },

    id: 8,
    name: 'EventHub',
    summary: 'An event discovery and booking platform for local communities.',
    description: 'A full-stack event management application where users can discover events, reserve tickets, manage bookings and receive event notifications.',
    projectLink: 'https://github.com/example/eventhub',
    tags: [Tag.ANGULAR, Tag.TYPESCRIPT, Tag.NODEJS],
    pictures: []
  },
  {
    id: 9,
    name: 'DevOps Monitor',
    summary: 'A dashboard for monitoring application health and deployment activity.',
    description: 'A monitoring dashboard that aggregates application metrics, deployment status, service availability and operational logs into a centralised interface.',
    projectLink: 'https://github.com/example/devops-monitor',
    tags: [Tag.REACT, Tag.TYPESCRIPT, Tag.PYTHON],
    pictures: []
  }
];

  constructor() { }

  getProjects(){
    return this.projects;
  }

  getProjectById(id: number): Project {
    let project = this.projects.find(project => project.id === id );

    if(project === undefined){
      throw new TypeError('There is no project that matches the id: ' + id);
    }

      return project;
  }

  getProjectsByFilter(filterTags: Tag[]): Project[] {

    if (filterTags.length === 0) {
        return this.getProjects();
    }

    return this.projects.filter(project =>
        project.tags.some(projectTag =>
            filterTags.includes(projectTag)
        )
    );
  }
}
